# Test hygiene, part 2

Planning baseline: `f3cb1565c17a30121b8ff1bc202883ece774b385`. Feature: `codex/test-hygiene-2`. Implemented cleanup commit: `93022efab7dede4aa5aaeb3c85a4eef51c0ed5d0`.

This report is an audit and decision list, not permission to connect, repair or remove its recommendations. Application execution, rule facts, sources and tracked generated data are unchanged. The BA standalone writer change is the explicit item-4 exception. No mass rerun of the 409 tests was performed.

The class column is inherited exactly from part 1 `RETAINED_RESULTS.json.finalClassification`, with its `finalEvidence` log when a part-1 check was repaired. Most original executions used `8e9a9528`; code review here uses immutable `f3cb1565` links. Historical PASS is not a new run on this feature, and an assertion or timeout leaves its later checks NOT_RUN.

Primary evidence is outside Git at `E:/Warhammer/qa-evidence/test-hygiene-1` and `E:/Warhammer/qa-evidence/test-hygiene-2`. The report preserves raw logs rather than replacing them with its one-line summaries. English summaries do not normalize rule values, source identities, recipient scopes or trace IDs.

### Python selection

`tests/helpers/qa-python.cjs` exports `resolveQaPython`: explicit CLI override, `QA_PYTHON`, caller-preserved legacy environment keys, current user Codex runtime, then `python` on PATH. Invalid explicit commands remain failures rather than silently falling back. No factual producer imports this helper.

The current base has 19 hardcoded Python test callers, not the suggested 23. All 19 were converted; counts were derived from tracked code. Two test-only Codex Node dependency paths are not Python selectors and were left untouched.

All 19 classes and first-error semantics are unchanged. Eighteen failure/PASS transcripts compare directly; the sole error-text normalization replaces an owned temporary-directory nonce in two ENOENT stack lines. It never alters error assertions or gameplay data. Six resolver controls pass, including explicit invalid override rejection and Codex/PATH fallback.

One initial clean-rebuild invocation used an invalid `Genetor` case filter. It is excluded, not accepted as baseline evidence. The 18 valid earlier baseline executions were reused; only that invalid invocation was repeated with the verified existing `Strafing Run` case. Its original invalid raw log was not retained because the corrective controller reused its pathname; `PYTHON_INVALID_INVOCATION.json` records this limitation. All 38 valid before/after results and logs are in `PYTHON_AB_VERIFIED_RESULTS.json`.

## 2. BA standalone output ownership

| Path or consumer | Finding |
| --- | --- |
| `generated/native-datasheets.json` / `datasheet-presentation.json` | No named reader, config input, test dependency or runtime allowlist entry. Neither is tracked. |
| `owned-book-view.mjs:4,79` | Consumes `buildNativeDatasheets()` in memory, not either file. |
| `book-build.mjs` / `app-build.mjs` | Use native/mixed-owner in-memory models and actual mixed-owner publications. |
| Runtime packager | Explicit manifest includes actual BA app/roster inputs, neither standalone file. |
| Generic source-closure scan | Requires exactly 97 generated JSON files. Additional outputs fail the inventory assertion before JSON contents are consumed; this is not a dependency on the pair. |

Normal `datasheet-build.mjs` invocation now returns/prints the in-memory adapter without emitting the unused pair. `--check` still validates the actual mixed-owner publication and all existing adapter assertions. Both invocations passed; neither file appeared and all 109 generated/cache files retained exact bytes. Runtime package parity is 438/438 files, SHA-256 `1605c22b7f5a7a32551d28cefdcca72fd92e41339f0cf227e24f41b32f27d9db`.

## 3. Architecture cluster: preserve live guards, split retired adapters

Do not delete the entire architecture cluster as if every source/canonical guard were obsolete. Retain live source, identity, editorial, publication and fact-direction controls; retire V1 provider/reader/Guides wiring, remove mappings to absent old leaf scripts after owner approval, and rebuild wrapper claims around actually executed current native checks. No changes are made in this task.

| Command | Actual meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `architecture:report` | node tests/architecture-conformance-gate.mjs --report; Runs the same child set, prints failures, and suppresses failure exit only through --report; report mode is not PASS. |
| `architecture:check` | node tests/architecture-conformance-gate.mjs; Same report with failure exit enabled. |
| `qa:architecture` | npm run wiring:check; Separate current wiring smoke, not this legacy aggregate cluster. |

There are 15 mapped contracts, 29 unique child paths and 21 named ASTRA labels. The wrapper runs one global child set and repeats each contract verdict for the nine public book rows; this is not 135 independently scoped semantic proofs. The part-1 raw log contains all nine rows: stdout/stderr interleave places four rows at lines 330-333, followed by `BOOKS=9` at 334. A completed wrapper or report-mode exit zero does not make failed children or their unexecuted tails PASS.

Do not delete the entire cluster. Preserve live source authenticity/lifecycle, canonical/scoped identity, factual direction, editorial and publication guards. Remove or replace only retired V1 adapter/mapping fragments after an independently reviewed split. Missing old child modules, external BSData, live producer defects and stale fixtures are distinct causes; no single broad exception should turn them into PASS. Both architecture aliases and all cluster source files are unchanged in this task.

## 4. Frozen 409-test cohort: recommendations only

| PASS | ASSERT | OTHER | TIMEOUT |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 144 | 111 | 149 | 5 |

| CONNECT | REPAIR | DEFECT | DELETE |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 140 | 241 | 14 | 14 |

`CONNECT` names a proposed existing queue and requires owner approval/deduplication. `REPAIR` is the next oracle/fixture/invocation investigation, never permission to replace EXPECTED with actual output. `DEFECT` means a suspected live production/build blocker, with confidence stated below, not a demonstrated new gameplay regression. `DELETE` identifies a retired protocol, not merely an old filename/URL. Mixed tests retain portable factual/source/physical guards.

Nine CONNECT rows already execute through mandatory imports/calls and need no extra leaf: `books/adeptus-mechanicus/v2/native-fnp-qa.mjs`, `books/chaos-space-marines/v2/critical-hit-threshold-qa.mjs`, `books/death-guard/v2/critical-hit-qa.mjs`, `books/tyranids/v2/seven-effects-qa.mjs`, `books/tyranids/v2/roster-ui-qa.mjs`, `v2/aggregate-physical-inventory-qa.mjs`, `v2/charge-effects-qa.mjs`, `v2/native-reference-presentation-qa.mjs`, `books/shared/source-refs-v2/source-closure-qa.mjs`. Thirteen Core tests already belong to the optional `qa:core` alias; that is not the same as mandatory pre-FF coverage. Adding the whole alias requires review of all its leaves.

The static import graph also reaches `personal-datasheet-qa.mjs` through `card-display-2-qa.mjs`, but only its fixture factory is used; its CLI-gated independent guards are not thereby executed. Reachability alone is not asserted as complete coverage.

TIMEOUT rows preserve the original part-1 180-second cap. In particular, DG/AM browser/rebuild tests still printed real passing activity shortly before termination; they are not diagnosed as hung or defective from that evidence. Parameterized tools and one-off A/B checks need valid invocation metadata; they must not be added bare to a mandatory queue.

The five Glossary rows share one encoded-versus-decoded freshness comparison. Seven general-points rows share the known SM Detachment canonical-join blocker. One Related Rules row has an accepted-input pin/history inconsistency requiring audit, not automatic rehashing. The DG allocation failure is explicitly unconfirmed: the raw log has no stack and large synthetic-census pressure may be responsible. These are not 14 independent gameplay defects. No producer, accepted source, pin or deployed card is changed.

A potential Plague Drone equipment defect was withdrawn on code review: the browser test reuses persisted state, clicks Import without confirming replacement, and accepts the already-true `units.length === 2` predicate. The current atomic replacement contract leaves the old aggregate state untouched. This is a stale test flow to repair, not evidence that current MODEL equipment was lost.

## 5. Verification and decision boundary

The pre-FF plan remains exactly 66 jobs. Default quick remains 28 jobs; all-book quick remains 48. package.json is byte-unchanged. Python test classes and first-error semantics are unchanged in the 19 before/after pairs. BA normal/check modes pass without new artifacts. All 109 generated/cache files and all 438 packaged files are byte-identical to baseline.

Run-specific final pre-FF status is recorded outside Git in `E:/Warhammer/qa-evidence/test-hygiene-2/preff/RESULTS.json`, `PREFF_CLASSIFIED_RESULTS.json` and the delivery report. Its outcomes are not substituted into these historical 409 rows. No extra full run, queue expansion, source refresh, gameplay repair, data change or fast-forward is authorized by this planning table. Source/main/release/Pages remain outside the feature push.
